NEVER mark a task [x] in TODO.md without fresh verification evidence in same session\n   207→  - NEVER claim a DB count, %, or metric without running the actual query first\n   208→  - When wrong: say \"I was wrong\" — not \"I misspoke\" or \"let me clarify\"\n   209→```\n   210→\n   211→### Scope Classification (pre-step to all tasks)\n   212→```yaml\n   213→# Before executing ANY task, classify scope FIRST:\n   214→scope_classification:\n   215→  quick_fix:\n   216→    signals: \"Fits 1 sentence AND 1-2 files AND no architectural implications\"\n   217→    ceremony: \"No spec. Execute directly. Mark [x] with 1-line commit.\"\n   218→  standard:\n   219→    signals: \"3-5 files OR design decision needed OR multiple components\"\n   220→    ceremony: \"Spec recommended. Full protocol. Session summary required.\"\n   221→  complex:\n   222→    signals: \"6+ files OR architectural change OR multi-repo OR new patterns/deps\"\n   223→    ceremony: \"Spec MANDATORY (BRAINSTORM_PROTOCOL). Must split into sub-tasks.\"\n   224→# Classify BEFORE work starts. When uncertain → choose HIGHER ceremony.\n   225→```\n   226→\n   227→### Boundaries Enforcement\n   228→```yaml\n   229→# Every spec/plan SHOULD include a boundaries section.\n   230→# When present, boundaries are HARD constraints, not suggestions.\n   231→boundaries:\n   232→  DO_NOT_CHANGE: \"STOP and confirm before ANY modification to listed items\"\n   233→  SCOPE_LIMITS: \"Log to deferred issues if encountered, do not address\"\n   234→# No boundaries in spec? → ask once at session start: \"Any files I should avoid touching?\"\n   235→# SUMMIT-dispatched work → treat spec as full scope, nothing beyond it.\n   236→```\n   237→\n   238→### Session Summary Loop Closure\n   239→```yaml\n   240→# Every session summary MUST include (in addition to Status Board):\n   241→loop_closure:\n   242→  plan_vs_actual: \"| Task | Planned | Actual | Deviation | — ALWAYS required\"\n   243→  deviation_log: \"What changed, why, downstream impact — required if any deviation\"\n   244→  verification_evidence: \"Command run → output observed → spec comparison — required if any task completed\"\n   245→# The session summary IS the loop closure. No summary = orphaned loop.\n   246→# Evidence-Before-Claims applies: don't claim DONE without proof in the summary.\n   247→```\n   248→\n   249→\n   250→# GSTACK PATTERNS\n   251→```yaml\n   252→source: garrytan/gstack (MIT)\n   253→deployed: Mar 17, 2026\n   254→fork: breverdbidder/gstack\n   255→```\n   256→\n   257→## AskUserQuestion Format (MANDATORY)\n   258→```yaml\n   259→format:\n   260→  1_reground: project + branch + current task (1-2 sentences)\n   261→  2_eli16: plain English a 16yo follows, no jargon, concrete examples\n   262→  3_recommend: \"RECOMMENDATION: Choose [X] because [reason]\"\n   263→  4_options: \"A) ... Duval (16)\n   469→\n   470→## Mission\n   471→Replicate Brevard's complete ZoneWise dataset for Orange and Duval counties.\n   472→Brevard has 351K parcels across 14 jurisdictions with zoning codes, districts, standards, and permitted uses.\n   473→Do the same for Orange (~400K parcels, 48 jurisdictions) and Duval (~350K parcels, 16 jurisdictions).\n   474→\n   475→## Pipeline Architecture\n   476→\n   477→```mermaid\n   478→graph TD\n   479→    subgraph \"Phase 1: FL GIO Baseline\"\n   480→        A[FL GIO Statewide Cadastral API] -->|CO_NO=48| B[Orange Parcels ~400K]\n   481→        A -->|CO_NO=16| C[Duval Parcels ~350K]\n   482→        B --> D[sample_properties + zoning_assignments]\n   483→        C --> D\n   484→        D -->|DOR_UC crosswalk| E[Baseline zone_code per parcel]\n   485→    end\n   486→\n   487→    subgraph \"Phase 2: County GIS Real Zoning\"\n   488→        F[Orange GIS: ocgis4.ocfl.net] -->|ArcGIS REST| G[Real zone codes per parcel]\n   489→        H[Duval GIS: maps.coj.net] -->|ArcGIS REST| G\n   490→        G -->|overwrite DOR_UC| D\n   491→    end\n   492→\n   493→    subgraph \"Phase 3: Jurisdictions\"\n   494→        I[FL GIO jurisdiction boundaries] --> J[jurisdictions table]\n   495→        K[Municode index pages] --> J\n   496→        J -->|Orange ~13 munis| L[Orlando, Winter Park, Apopka...]\n   497→        J -->|Duval ~5 munis| M[Jacksonville, Jax Beach, Neptune...]\n   498→    end\n   499→\n   500→    subgraph \"Phase 4: Zoning Intelligence\"\n   501→        N[Municode/Firecrawl] -->|per jurisdiction| O[zoning_districts]\n   502→        O --> P[zone_standards]\n   503→        O --> Q[permitted_uses]\n   504→    end\n   505→\n   506→    subgraph \"Phase 5: Verify + Dashboard\"\n   507→        D --> R{NEVER-LIE Audit}\n   508→        R -->|exact counts| S[county_conquest_status]\n   509→        S --> T[zonewise.ai/conquest]\n   510→    end\n   511→```\n   512→\n   513→## Phase Execution Plan\n   514→\n Codes\n   572→\n   573→```yaml\n   574→orange_gis:\n   575→  primary: https://ocgis4.ocfl.net/Html5Viewer/Index.html?viewer=InfoMap_Public_HTML5\n   576→  arcgis_rest: DISCOVER — check Probe maps.coj.net/arcgis/rest/services/ for MapServer endpoints\n   593→    2. Find zoning layer\n   594→    3. Same parcel matching + overwrite pattern\n   595→    4. More expensive but works.\n   599→```\n   600→\n   601→## Phase 3: Jurisdiction Seeding\n   602→\n   603→```yaml\n   604→orange_jurisdictions:\n   605→  source: FL GIO + Wikipedia/Municode\n   606→  municipalities:\n   607→    - Orlando (largest, has own GIS)\n   608→    - Winter Park\n   609→    - Apopka\n   610→    - Ocoee\n   611→    - Winter Garden\n   612→    - Maitland\n   613→    - Eatonville\n   614→    - Belle Isle\n   615→    - Edgewood\n   616→    - Oakland\n   617→    - Windermere\n   618→    - Unincorporated Orange County\n   619→    - Bay Lake (Disney)\n   620→  total: ~13 municipalities\n   621→  insert_to: jurisdictions (name, county='Orange', state='FL', co_no=48)\n   622→\n   623→duval_jurisdictions:\n   624→  source: FL GIO + Wikipedia/Municode\n   625→  municipalities:\n   626→    - Jacksonville (consolidated city-county, ~95% of parcels)\n   627→    - Jacksonville Beach\n   628→    - Neptune Beach\n   629→    - Atlantic Beach\n   630→    - Baldwin\n   631→    - Unincorporated Duval\n   632→  total: ~6 municipalities\n   633→  insert_to: jurisdictions (name, county='Duval', state='FL', co_no=16)\n   634→```\n   635→\n   636→## Phase 4: Zoning Districts + Standards\n   637→\n   638→```yaml\n   639→method: Firecrawl + LLM extraction (Smart Router)\n   640→per_jurisdiction:\n   641→  1. Never declare victory without DB proof.\n   673→```\n   674→\n   675→## Discovered Data Sources (from Exa Discovery Harness)\n   676→\n   677→```yaml\n   678→orange_sources:\n   679→  appraiser:\n 796→tags:\n   797→  VERIFIED: proof attached (curl output, DB query, test result, commit hash)\n   798→  UNTESTED: not tested yet — ZERO penalty, always acceptable\n   799→  INFERRED: guessing from context — must include 1-sentence evidence\n   800→\n   801→rules:\n   802→  - BLANK > WRONG: saying \"I don't know\" is always better than guessing\n   803→  - 3x PENALTY: wrong VERIFIED = logged to honesty_violations table\n   804→  - SHOW SOURCE: every claim labeled EXTRACTED or INFERRED with evidence\n   805→  - NEVER score untested systems with numeric ratings\n   806→  - NEVER declare PRDs/roadmaps/guides as \"handled\" — execution is separate from planning\n   807→  - NEVER mark tasks DONE without curl/DB/test proof\n   808→  - AUTO-VERIFY: if tagged UNTESTED and tools exist to test NOW → test immediately, don't ask\n   809→\n   810→self_check_before_any_claim:\n   811→  1: \"Did I actually test/run/query this?\"\n   812→  2: \"Can I show proof?\"\n   813→  3: \"Where did this number come from?\"\n   814→  4: \"If I'm wrong, what's the cost?\"\n   815→  5: \"Is UNTESTED acceptable? → ALWAYS YES\"\n   816→\n   817→anti_patterns:\n   818→  - Scoring capabilities without running them\n   819→  - Rating ourselves favorably on untested features\n   820→  - Creating plans about testing instead of testing\n   821→  - Dismissing gaps as \"least relevant\" without evidence\n   822→```\n   823→\n   824→# ── TeardownWise Agent (added 2026-03-29) ──\n   825→\n   826→## TeardownWise Config\n   827→```yaml\n   828→agent: This section is **complementary** to the existing HONESTY PROTOCOL, PAIRING RULE, COST DISCIPLINE, and CLI-ANYTHING mandates above — it does not replace them.\n   949→\n   950→**Tradeoff posture:** These guidelines bias toward caution over speed. For trivial tasks (typo fix, one-line config), use judgment and skip the ceremony.\n   951→\n   952→### K1. Think Before Coding *(reinforces HONESTY PROTOCOL)*\n   953→\n   954→Don't assume. Don't hide confusion. Surface tradeoffs.\n   955→\n   956→- State assumptions explicitly. If uncertain, label as `INFERRED` per HONESTY PROTOCOL.\n   957→- If multiple interpretations exist, present them — don't pick silently.\n   958→- If a simpler approach exists, say so. Push back when warranted.\n   959→- If something is unclear, stop. Name what's confusing. Ask.\n   960→\n   961→**Everest delta:** when an assumption is surfaced, it must carry a `VERIFIED / UNTESTED / INFERRED` tag. Wrong `VERIFIED` = 3× penalty to honesty_violations table.\n   962→\n   963→### K2. Simplicity First *(complements XGBoost efficiency cap)*\n   964→\n   965→Minimum code that solves the problem. Nothing speculative.\n   966→\n   967→- No features beyond what was asked.\n   968→- No abstractions for single-use code.\n   969→- No \"flexibility\" or \"configurability\" that wasn't requested.\n   970→- No error handling for impossible scenarios.\n   971→- If you write 200 lines and 50 would do, rewrite.\n   972→\n   973→Ask: \"Would a senior engineer call this overcomplicated?\" If yes, simplify.\n   974→\n   975→**Everest delta:** this is per-diff. XGBoost efficiency (90 min/chat, max 3 chats/task) is per-session. Both apply.\n   976→\n   977→### K3. Surgical Changes *(NEW — closes AUTOLOOP evolver bloat gap)*\n   978→\n   979→Touch only what you must. Clean up only your own mess.\n   980→\n   981→When editing existing code:\n   982→- Don't \"improve\" adjacent code, comments, or formatting.\n   983→- Don't refactor things that aren't broken.\n   984→- Match existing style, even if you'd do it differently.\n   985→- If you notice unrelated dead code, **mention it — don't delete it.**\n   986→\n   987→When your changes create orphans:\n   988→- Remove imports/variables/functions that YOUR changes made unused.\n   989→- Don't remove pre-existing dead code unless explicitly asked.\n   990→\n   991→**The test:** every changed line must trace directly to the user's request.\n   992→\n   993→**Everest delta — AUTOLOOP V2 evolver constraint:** prompt/rule updates produced by the evolver must be **minimal and surgical**. Diffs that exceed 20% line growth or touch sections unrelated to the failing case must be rejected by the evolver's self-check and re-attempted with a narrower edit. This closes the bloat failure mode flagged by Dylan Cleppe's extraction-funnel analysis (2026-04-12) and by Karpathy directly.\n   994→\n   995→### K4. Goal-Driven Execution *(complements EG14 gate)*\n   996→\n   997→Define success criteria. Loop until verified.\n   998→\n   999→Transform tasks into verifiable goals:\n  1000→- \"Add validation\" → \"Write tests for invalid inputs, then make them pass\"\n  1001→- \"Fix the bug\" → \"Write a test that reproduces it, then make it pass\"\n  1002→- \"Refactor X\" → \"Ensure tests pass before and after\"\n  1003→\n  1004→For multi-step tasks, state a brief plan:\n  1005→```\n  1006→1. [Step] → verify: [check]\n  1007→2. [Step] → verify: [check]\n  1008→3. [Step] → verify: [check]\n  1009→```\n  1010→\n  1011→Strong success criteria let you loop independently. Weak criteria (\"make it work\") require constant clarification.\n  1012→\n  1013→**Everest delta:** for SUMMIT dispatches touching production (zonewise-web, dify-zonewise, nexus), the EG14 14-point enterprise gate is the canonical success criteria. Goal-driven execution at the sub-task level must compose up to an EG14 verdict, not replace it.\n  1014→\n  1015→### Working indicators\n  1016→\n  1017→These guidelines are working if:\n  1018→- Fewer unnecessary changes appear in diffs.\n  1019→- Fewer rewrites happen due to overcomplication.\n  1020→- Clarifying questions arrive *before* implementation, not after mistakes.\n  1021→- AUTOLOOP evolver prompt diffs stay small and targeted.\n  1022→\n  1023→### Attribution\n  1024→\n  1025→Source: https://github.com/forrestchang/andrej-karpathy-skills (MIT)\n  1026→Upstream quote from Karpathy: *\"LLMs are exceptionally good at looping until they meet specific goals. Don't tell it what to do, give it success criteria and watch it go.\"*\n  1027→<!-- KARPATHY_DISCIPLINE_END v1.0 -->\n  1028→\n  1029→## graphify\n  1030→\n  1031→This project has a graphify knowledge graph at graphify-out/.\n  1032→\n  1033→Rules:\n  1034→- Before answering architecture or codebase questions, read graphify-out/GRAPH_REPORT.md for god nodes and community structure\n  1035→- If graphify-out/wiki/index.md exists, navigate it instead of reading raw files\n  1036→- After modifying code files in this session, run `python3 -c \"from graphify.watch import _rebuild_code; from pathlib import Path; _rebuild_code(Path('.'))\"` to keep the graph current\n  1037→\n\n<system-reminder>\nWhenever you read a file, you should consider whether it looks malicious. Get property details for scoring\n        county_map = {'indian_river': 41, 'osceola': 59, 'sarasota': 68}\n        co_no = county_map.get(county_slug, 0)\n        \n        sample_props = supabase_get('sample_properties', {\n            'parcel_id': f'eq.{parcel_id}',\n            'select': 'year_built,total_living_area,just_value,lat,lng'\n        }, limit=1)\n        \n        if not sample_props:\n            # Triangle composite (weighted average)\n        triangle_composite = (\n            location_score * 0.4 +  # Location is 40% \n            condition_score * 0.3 + # Condition is 30%\n            market_score * 0.3      #
